Moreover, to deal with the case where elicitation terminates before the ambiguity set reduces to a singleton, we demonstrate how to figure out a nominal utility function by solving optimization programs. These identify the smallest and largest utility functions under the Kantorovich metric within the ambiguity set, after which we identify a nominal utility function located in the middle of them. Finally, numerical results demonstrate the efficiency of the MUS method and the performance of a robo-advisor system based on MUS-type queries and the nominal utility elicitation. While the main discussions focus on concave utility functions, we also demonstrate how the MUS approach can be extended to accommodate general non-concave utility functions, particularly S-shaped ones.

</details>


### [113] [Randomized Optimal Switching Problem and Related Mirror Descent Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12875)
*Yuchao Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间强化学习在最优切换问题中的应用，通过KL散度正则化的随机化框架松弛经典确定性切换控制，证明正则化值函数是椭圆HJB系统的唯一光滑解，给出指数变换形式的显式最优Gibbs策略，并建立了正则化逼近误差的$O(λ\log\frac1λ)$上界。进一步提出对数策略空间上的镜像下降流算法，在常温和退火温度调度下分别给出收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间最优切换问题在工程、金融等领域有广泛应用，但经典确定性控制框架下缺乏有效的探索机制，且数值求解困难。本文旨在通过熵正则化引入随机性，使得在保持问题可解性的同时实现探索，并为数值求解提供可收敛的算法框架。

Method: 将经典确定性最优切换问题松弛为连续时间马尔可夫链驱动的随机化框架，引入KL散度正则化项（温度参数λ加权）。证明正则化值函数满足椭圆HJB系统并存在唯一光滑解，推导出指数变换形式的显式最优Gibbs策略。在数值求解方面，提出对数策略空间上的镜像下降流，并分析常温和退火$λ_s=1/\sqrt{1+s}$两种温度调度下的收敛率。

Result: 1) 正则化值函数是椭圆HJB系统的唯一光滑解，最优策略为Gibbs分布形式；2) 正则化逼近经典最优值函数的误差为$O(λ\log\frac1λ)$；3) 镜像下降流单调递减值函数，常温调度下收敛率$O(1/(e^{λs}-1)+λ\log\frac1λ)$，退火调度下$O(\log s/\sqrt{s})$。

Conclusion: 本文为连续时间最优切换问题提供了理论完备的熵正则化框架，兼具可解性和可探索性，所提镜像下降流算法在常温和退火调度下均能收敛到经典最优值函数，且退火调度下误差随流时间趋于零。

Abstract: We study continuous-time reinforcement learning for the optimal switching problem, in which a decision-maker controls a diffusion process by switching among finitely many regimes, incurring both running and transition costs. To enable exploration, we relax the classical deterministic switching control to a randomized framework, where the switching decisions are governed by a continuous-time Markov chain with state-dependent generator, and augment the cost functional with a KL-divergence regularization weighted by a temperature parameter $λ$. Under mild assumptions on the coefficients, we establish that the regularized value function is the unique smooth solution of an elliptic Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman system, and derive an explicit optimal Gibbs policy given by an exponential transformation of the value function differences across modes. We further prove that the regularized value function approximates the classical optimal value function with error of order $O\left(λ\log \frac{1}λ\right)$, which is consistent with analogous bounds established in other entropy-regularized control problems and is believed to be sharp. To solve the regularized problem numerically, we introduce a mirror descent flow in the dual logarithmic policy space, prove its well-posedness and the monotonic decrease of the value function along the flow, and establish quantitative error bound to the classical optimal value function. For a constant temperature scheduler, the convergence rate is of order $O\left(\frac{1}{e^{λs} - 1}+λ\log\frac1λ\right)$, while under the annealing scheduler $λ_s = \frac{1}{\sqrt{1+s}}$, we obtain the rate $O\left(\frac{\log s}{\sqrt{s}}\right)$, which decays to zero as the flow time $s \to \infty$.

</details>


### [114] [Flyby Distance Pursuit for Guarding a Target with an Inferior Guard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13012)
*Navot Israeli,Vitaly Shaferman,Oded Golan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于微分博弈框架研究慢速防御者（Guard）拦截快速攻击者（Attacker）的目标保护问题。通过引入记录最小距离的辅助状态变量，将博弈值定义为飞越距离，结合终端约束求解两点边值问题，发现攻击者仅需到达目标时可能不被捕获，但施加终端速度约束可实现捕获并揭示速度与飞越距离的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追击博弈存在结构限制，无法有效处理攻击者必须到达目标且防御者较慢的场景。作者希望通过新的博弈公式化方法突破这一局限，并嵌入攻击任务约束。

Method: 将问题建模为微分博弈，引入记录最小分离距离的辅助状态变量使博弈值（飞越距离）在终端可计算；采用间接法构建两点边值问题（TPBVP）并进行数值求解。

Result: 识别出多种解类型：仅约束攻击者到达目标时，攻击者可避免被捕获但飞行时间过长、终端速度过低；施加终端速度约束后，解更简单，在相关参数空间内可实现捕获，并揭示了终端速度与飞越距离间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提方法消除了当前追击博弈的结构性限制，开辟了一类新的可解问题。施加攻击者终端速度约束是获得实用解的关键，能够在防御者较慢的条件下实现捕获。

Abstract: Guarding a target against a fast Attacker with a slower Guard is posed as a differential game. Both players follow nonlinear, thrust-free, aerodynamic motion in the plane. The capturability problem inherent in this scenario is addressed by formulating the game such that the Attacker is constrained to terminate at its Target, and the game value is the flyby distance. The latter is made accessible at termination by introducing an auxiliary state variable that records the players' minimum separation distance throughout their flight. This novel combination of termination condition and auxiliary state removes a structural limitation of current pursuit games and opens a new class of solvable problems. It also enables us to embed attacker mission constraints directly. Taking an indirect approach, we formulate the game's Two-Point Boundary Value Problem, solve it numerically, and identify several solution types. When the Attacker's only constraint is to reach the Target, he can do so without being captured. These types of solutions, however, are less practical due to long flight times and slow terminal speeds. Imposing an Attacker terminal speed constraint yields simpler solutions and reveals the trade-off between terminal speed and flyby distance. We demonstrate that in such a setting, capture is possible in a relevant region of the parameter space.

</details>


### [115] [A Survey of Community Detection from an Operations Research Perspective: Taxonomy, Mathematical Formulations, Modularity Functions, and Benchmark Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13031)
*Miguel A. Pozo Montaño,Sebastián V Taboh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了社区检测问题的统一研究框架，重点综述了基于模块度的社区检测方法。文章构建了多维分类体系，建立了包含非重叠、重叠和模糊社区结构的形式化数学模型，并系统评述了模块度函数、求解方法及基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社区检测文献缺乏统一的概念框架和分类标准，导致研究碎片化。作者旨在通过整合分类学、数学建模、模块度分析和基准测试实践，为研究人员和 practitioners 提供结构化参考。

Method: (1) 提出多维分类法，按网络特征、社区结构、目标函数、方法论范式、评估准则和应用领域组织方法；(2) 建立统一的数学形式化框架，兼容非重叠、重叠和模糊社区结构；(3) 系统评述代表性模块度函数及其假设、空模型和局限；(4) 区分算法方法和数学规划方法；(5) 综述基准数据集及其在评估与可重复性中的作用。

Result: 构建了一个整合分类学、数学模型、模块度分析和基准测试的综合性综述框架，为社区检测研究提供了结构化参考体系。

Conclusion: 该综述通过统一框架整合了社区检测的多维方面，填补了现有文献缺乏共同概念基础的空白，为未来研究提供了系统化指导。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental problem in network science that consists of identifying groups of vertices exhibiting stronger internal connectivity than external connectivity. From an Operations Research perspective, the problem can be interpreted as a family of combinatorial optimization and clustering models defined over network structures. This survey provides a unified framework for the study of community detection, with particular emphasis on modularity-based approaches. We first review existing surveys and analyze the taxonomic criteria used to classify the literature, highlighting the absence of a common conceptual framework. Based on this analysis, we propose a multidimensional taxonomy that organizes community detection methods according to network characteristics, community structure, objective functions, methodological paradigms, evaluation criteria, and application domains. We then introduce a general mathematical formalization of the Community Detection Problem that accommodates disjoint, overlapping, and fuzzy community structures within a unified assignment framework. Building on this formalization, we review representative modularity functions, discussing their underlying assumptions, null models, and known limitations. We also survey modularity-based community detection methods, distinguishing between algorithmic and mathematical programming approaches. Finally, we review commonly used benchmark datasets and discuss their role in evaluation and reproducibility. By integrating taxonomy, mathematical modeling, modularity analysis, and benchmarking practices, this survey provides a structured reference for researchers and practitioners working on community detection and related network optimization problems.

</details>


### [116] [Scheduling of Star Observations under Uncertain Conditions: A Comparison of Models and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13058)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究望远镜观测恒星调度问题，将夜晚视为机器、恒星视为作业，在不确定天气条件（完美或糟糕夜晚）下，采用鲁棒优化方法评估最坏情况，最大化观测总利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际天文观测中，气象和大气条件在制定调度计划时未知，传统确定性调度模型无法应对这种不确定性，因此需要一种在不确知实际可用夜晚数量下仍能保证高质量的调度方案。

Method: 将问题建模为平行机调度（夜晚=机器，恒星=作业），每个作业有可见时间窗和最小观测时长约束；天气不确定性简化为夜晚要么完美（可观测）要么极差（不可观测），在鲁棒优化框架下评估调度方案的最坏情况表现。

Result: 在鲁棒优化范式下，给出了针对不确定可用机器数量的调度问题在最坏情况下的性能评估结果。

Conclusion: 通过鲁棒优化方法，可以在不确知实际天气条件的情况下，为望远镜观测调度提供具有最坏情况保障的高质量方案。

Abstract: We consider a scheduling problem for star observations using a telescope. Identical nights are available to observe stars that have been identified as of interest by astronomers. Each star yields a profit if it is observed; in that case, the observation must respect a visibility window and a minimum observation duration. The objective is to maximize the total profit of the observations that are actually performed. By interpreting nights as machines and stars as jobs to be processed, this problem is a classic scheduling setting of parallel machines. In the practical star-observation problem, the ability to observe depends on meteorological and atmospheric conditions, which are not known when the schedule is computed. A possible modeling is to consider that each night can be either perfect and everything is observable, either terrible and nothing is observable. The challenge is therefore to propose a ''high-quality'' schedule without knowing the actual number of nights (machines) that will ultimately be available. We focus here on evaluating the worst case within the robust optimization paradigm.

</details>


### [117] [A symmetric Gauss-Seidel based alternating proximal ALM for generalized Nash Equilibrium problems in Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13198)
*Defeng Sun,Hailing Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对单调广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）的新型对称高斯-赛德尔交替近端增广拉格朗日方法（sGS-APALM），该方法通过交替更新玩家策略和设计二次替代函数，在仅需伪梯度映射单调性和Lipschitz连续的弱假设下实现全局收敛，并成功应用于风险中性PDE约束GNEP问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正则化和增广拉格朗日方法在处理单调GNEP时，要么需要在每次迭代中求解耦合的纳什均衡子问题（计算成本高），要么需要强单调性或共强制等强假设才能保证收敛。本文旨在开发一种在弱假设下高效收敛的交替分裂方法。

Method: 提出sGS-APALM方法：基于对称高斯-赛德尔（sGS）技巧，设计新的二次替代函数，在每次迭代中交替更新各玩家的策略，将原问题分解为一系列无约束二次规划子问题，避免了耦合子问题的求解。

Result: 在仅需伪梯度映射单调性和Lipschitz连续的假设下建立了全局收敛性并给出了收敛率估计；应用于风险中性PDE约束GNEP的初步数值实验验证了方法的效率和有效性。

Conclusion: sGS-APALM是一种在弱假设下对单调GNEP具有全局收敛保证的高效交替分裂算法，为求解带有联合约束的无穷维博弈问题提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a class of monotone generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) with jointly linear constraints. The players' strategy spaces are real Hilbert spaces, while the joint constraint is formulated in a Banach space. To solve such problems, we propose a novel symmetric Gauss-Seidel (sGS) based alternating proximal augmented Lagrangian method (sGS-APALM) which incorporates newly designed quadratic surrogates. In contrast to existing regularization and ALM-type methods, the proposed method avoids solving coupled Nash equilibrium subproblems at each iteration and instead updates the players' strategies alternately by solving a sequence of unconstrained quadratic programs. Moreover, unlike many existing splitting-based methods, our global convergence analysis and convergence rate estimation require only monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the pseudo-gradient mapping, without imposing stronger assumptions such as strong monotonicity or co-coercivity. Finally, we apply the method to a class of risk-neutral PDE-constrained GNEPs with joint state constraints, and preliminary numerical results demonstrate its efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [118] [A smoothing extended sequential quadratic method for difference-of-convex optimization over a convex composite inequality constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13343)
*Jiefeng Xu,Ting Kei Pong,Yongle Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的ESQM算法，通过引入可变平滑策略求解凸复合不等式约束和紧凸集约束下的差凸优化问题，建立了$O(ε^{-3})$的迭代复杂度以获得$(ε,ε)$-KKT点，并在凸情形下证明了全序列收敛性及Hölderian增长条件下的局部收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解带有凸复合不等式约束和紧凸集约束的差凸优化问题，现有算法在该类问题上的理论保证不足。

Method: 在ESQM基础上引入可变平滑机制，每轮迭代对基于凸复合约束函数光滑近似构造的平滑罚函数执行一步近端梯度更新，并设计显式规则更新平滑参数和罚参数。

Result: 在约束规格下，获得$(ε,ε)$-KKT点的迭代复杂度为$O(ε^{-3})$；凸情形下全序列收敛，且在Hölderian增长条件下建立了局部收敛速率。

Conclusion: 扩展算法有效求解差凸约束优化问题，具有理论保证的全局复杂度和局部收敛性质。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing a difference-of-convex objective over a convex composite inequality constraint and a compact convex set constraint. To solve this problem, we extend the ESQM in [1] via incorporating a variable smoothing scheme. In essence, in each iteration of our algorithm, we apply one proximal gradient step to a smoothed penalty function, constructed based on a smooth approximation of the convex composite constraint function; and we design explicit rules to update the smoothing and penalty parameters. Under suitable constraint qualifications, we establish an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for obtaining an $(ε,ε)$-KKT point. Moreover, in the convex setting, we show that the whole sequence generated by our algorithm is convergent and derive its local convergence rate under a standard Hölderian growth condition.

</details>


### [119] [Coordinate-wise Polyhedral Method for Eliciting Multivariate Linear Utility and Univariate Nonlinear Utility Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13418)
*Jiaxin Wei,Jia Liu,Huifu Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种坐标式多面体方法（CPM），通过预先指定坐标轴方向切割并求解线性方程组来设计成对比较查询，实现了多面体直径的线性收敛率，并将其扩展至单变量分段线性及一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数的估计，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多面体方法通过求解耦合优化问题来设计成对比较查询，计算复杂且效率有待提升。本文旨在提出一种更高效的坐标式切割方法，在保证收敛性的同时简化查询设计过程。

Method: CPM预先指定坐标轴方向的切割，然后通过求解线性方程组来设计对应的成对比较查询。对于单变量分段线性效用函数，用增量向量表示；对于一般非递减Lipschitz连续效用函数，采用分段线性近似（PLA）并引入自适应断点策略以避免方向误差。

Result: CPM以线性收敛速率减少多面体直径；在单变量分段线性效用函数下，Kantorovich距离呈线性收敛；在一般非递减Lipschitz连续情形下，增量向量对应的分段线性效用函数集合随查询次数增加收敛到真实效用函数，并给出了显式近似误差界；数值实验验证了CPM在线性多变量和单变量非线性效用函数下的收敛性能。

Conclusion: CPM提供了一种高效的坐标式切割多面体方法，具有理论收敛保证，并能够有效扩展到一般非线性效用函数的估计，实验与理论结果一致。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a coordinate-wise polyhedral method (CPM) for cutting polyhedra with theoretical guarantees of convergence. Unlike the existing polyhedral method, which designs pairwise comparison queries by solving coupled optimization problems and performs cuts subsequently, CPM specifies coordinate-wise cuts in advance and then designs corresponding pairwise comparison queries by solving a linear system of equations. Under this framework, we show that CPM reduces the diameter of the polyhedron at a linear convergence rate. Moreover, we extend CPM to a univariate piecewise-linear utility function by representing it with its increment vector over consecutive breakpoints. We show that the Kantorovich distance between the true utility function and the estimated one obtained by CPM decreases at a linear convergence rate. Further, we extend the CPM to general nondecreasing Lipschitz continuous utility functions by piecewise-linear approximation (PLA). We introduce an adaptive-breakpoint strategy to avoid direction errors caused by PLA. We prove that the set of piecewise-linear utility functions corresponding to the ambiguity set of increment vectors converges to the true utility function as the number of queries increases, and derive an explicit bound for the approximation. Finally, to evaluate the performance of CPM, we conduct a series of numerical experiments. The results demonstrate comparable convergence to the standard polyhedral method in the case of linear multivariate utility functions. For nonlinear univariate utility functions, CPM achieves stable convergence to the true utility function, in line with the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [120] [Towards a Control interpretation of Quantum Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13481)
*Dario Pighin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于控制理论的框架来理解量子优势（QA），通过将量子计算重述为特殊酉群SU(N)上的算子可控性问题，并利用最小时间函数的多项式上界来识别QA。在量子傅里叶变换（超导数字量子处理器）和最大独立集问题（中性原子模拟量子处理器）两个范式问题上验证了该框架，分别证明了算子可控性并推导了时间上界，最后提出了控制理论视角下的量子优势定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对量子优势的理解多集中在具体算法或实验实现层面，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来系统性地刻画量子优势何时以及如何产生。控制理论作为研究系统可控性和最优控制的数学工具，有望为量子优势提供严谨的形式化描述。

Method: 1）将量子计算重述为双线性受控薛定谔方程下的算子可控性问题；2）在SU(N)群上定义最小时间函数，并将其多项式上界作为量子优势的标志；3）通过李代数论证证明算子可控性；4）利用门级联引理和标准电路分解推导时间上界；5）将QAOA重述为连续时间最优控制问题。

Result: 在超导数字量子处理器上，通过李代数论证证明了QFT的算子可控性，并推导出O(n²)的最小时间上界；在中性原子模拟量子处理器上，分析了里德伯阻塞哈密顿量作为双线性控制系统，证明了MIS问题的可控性，并提出了基于控制的量子优势定义。

Conclusion: 控制理论为理解量子优势提供了一个统一的数学框架，能够系统性地刻画量子优势的条件和界限，并在不同量子计算范式（数字与模拟）上均具有适用性。该工作为控制理论与量子计算的交叉研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: We develop a control-theoretic framework for understanding Quantum Advantage (QA), providing a systematic route to characterize when and how QA can arise. The bilinear controlled Schrödinger equation is the common thread: the target quantum computation is recast as an operator controllability problem on the special unitary group $SU(N)$, and QA is identified with a polynomial-in-$n$ upper bound on the associated minimal-time function.
  We illustrate the framework on two paradigmatic problems: a) the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT) on superconducting digital quantum processors (such as IBM's ibm_brisbane), for which we prove operator controllability by a Lie-algebraic argument and derive an $O(n^2)$ upper bound on the minimal time via a gate-concatenation lemma combined with the standard QFT circuit decomposition; b) the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem on neutral-atom analog quantum processors (such as Pasqal's hardware), for which we analyze the Rydberg-blockade Hamiltonian as a bilinear control system and reformulate the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) as a continuous-time optimal control problem. By a controllability result, we show how the problem can be solved on Pasqal Quantum Computers and we introduce a control-based definition of Quantum Advantage for MIS.
  We conclude by outlining several open problems that chart directions for future research at the intersection of Control Theory and Quantum Computing.

</details>


### [121] [A Sustainable Integrated Framework for Multi-Type Urban Waste Collection and Recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13524)
*Víctor Blanco,J. Fernando Camacho-Vallejo,Yolanda Hinojosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向城市多类型垃圾收集与回收系统的集成优化框架，联合决策中转设施与处理厂选址及双层路径规划，在有限预算下最小化运营总成本，并通过麦德林市真实场景验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市废物管理面临人口增长、垃圾种类异质、交通拥堵及可持续基础设施需求等日益严峻的挑战，现有方法未能有效整合设施选址与多产品双层路径规划问题。

Method: 构建了一个集成定位-路径模型（Location-Routing Model），同时考虑设施选址决策、多产品路径结构、车辆容量约束及重复卸载操作，目标是在投资预算约束下最小化运输、路径规划及处理等总运营成本。

Result: 基于哥伦比亚麦德林市真实城市场景的计算实验表明，集成化的基础设施与路径决策能够显著提升运营效率，支持更可持续的回收作业。

Conclusion: 该框架为城市规划者设计经济高效且环境友好的废物管理系统提供了实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Urban waste management faces increasing operational and environmental challenges driven by population growth, heterogeneous waste streams, traffic congestion, and the need for sustainable collection infrastructures. We present an integrated optimization framework for the design of multi-type urban waste collection and recycling systems. In the proposed network, waste generated at distributed collection points is gathered by waste-specific small vehicles and transported to intermediate consolidation facilities, where it is aggregated before being transferred to treatment plants using larger vehicles. The problem consists of jointly determining the location of consolidation facilities and treatment plants, together with the associated two-echelon routing operations, under a limited infrastructure budget. We develop an integrated location-routing model that simultaneously captures facility-location decisions, multi-product routing structures, vehicle-capacity restrictions, and repeated unloading operations. The objective is to minimize total operational costs, including transportation, routing, and handling activities, while satisfying investment-budget constraints. The framework promotes compact collection regions, improves vehicle utilization, and reduces unnecessary transportation effort in dense urban environments. Computational experiments based on realistic urban scenarios derived from the city of Medellín (Colombia) demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. The results show that integrated infrastructure and routing decisions can substantially improve operational efficiency while supporting more sustainable recycling operations. The framework provides a practical decision-support tool for urban planners seeking to design cost-efficient and environmentally responsible waste-management systems.

</details>


### [122] [Differential Geometric Conditions for Koopman Linearizability of Control-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13577)
*Shankar A. Deka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出控制仿射非线性系统可被Koopman线性化的微分几何条件，证明了漂移与控制向量场需满足的必要条件，以及在控制不变流形上该条件也是充分条件；额外条件则构成可控线性系统Koopman线性化的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定非线性控制系统是否存在有限维Koopman表示是一个关键问题，本文旨在刻画所有可Koopman线性化的非线性控制系统的类别，建立可线性化的充要条件。

Method: 采用微分几何方法，分析控制仿射系统的漂移向量场和控制向量场必须满足的条件；在控制不变流形上证明充分性；引入额外条件得到充要条件。

Result: 给出了控制仿射系统Koopman线性化的必要条件（对漂移和控制向量场的微分几何条件）；证明了这些条件在控制不变流形上也是充分的；加上额外条件后成为可控线性系统Koopman线性化的充要条件。

Conclusion: 即便系统的自治部分可通过Koopman提升线性化，整个控制仿射系统也可能不存在Koopman线性化变换，本文的几何条件易于检验，为Koopman线性化的存在性提供了清晰的判定准则。

Abstract: Koopman linearization opens many possibilities for control synthesis and analysis of nonlinear systems. Whether or not any given nonlinear control system admits a finite-dimensional Koopman representation remains a crucial question to address. A related problem is to categorize the class of all Koopman linearizable nonlinear control systems. In this work, we present differential geometric conditions on the drift and control vector fields of a control-affine nonlinear system, that must be necessarily satisfied for Koopman linear transformation to exist. The same conditions are also shown to be sufficient for (a slightly weaker notion of) Koopman linearizability on control-invariant manifolds. Further, these conditions, together with an additional condition, become necessary and sufficient for Koopman linearizability to a controllable linear system. Our examples illustrate the ease of checking these conditions, and also shed light on how Koopman linearizing transformation may not exist for a control-affine system even though one can linearize the autonomous part of the system via Koopman lifting.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal Proximity Bound and Product Function Estimates in Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13579)
*Iskander Aliev,Gennadiy Averkov,William Jones,Timm Oertel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了标准型整数线性规划中，LP松弛的最优顶点解到最近整数最优解的欧几里得距离上界为 $\sqrt{\det(AA^t)}-1$，且该界是渐近紧的。同时推导了涉及乘积函数 $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(x_i+1)$ 的整数最优解界限，并讨论了在背包问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为整数线性规划提供最优邻近性（proximity）界，即LP松弛解与整数最优解之间的最大距离，填补该领域精确界的空白。

Method: 利用矩阵A的Gram矩阵行列式 $\det(AA^t)$ 构造几何论证，结合整数规划的格结构推导欧几里得距离上界；通过乘积函数 $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(x_i+1)$ 推导新的整数解界限。

Result: 得到邻近性界 $\sqrt{\det(AA^t)}-1$，证明该界渐近最优；导出基于乘积函数的整数解界限，并展示在背包问题中的具体应用。

Conclusion: 给出了标准型整数线性规划中首个渐近紧的最优邻近性界，为整数规划解的结构分析提供了新的几何工具。

Abstract: We obtain an optimal proximity bound for integer linear programs in standard form max{cx: Ax=b, x nonnegative integer}, where A is an integer mxn matrix of rank m<n and b is an integer vector. Specifically, we show that the Euclidean distance from any optimal vertex solution of the LP relaxation to a nearest optimal integer solution is bounded by $\sqrt{\det(AA^t)}-1$ and that this estimate is asymptotically tight. We also derive bounds for the optimal integer solutions involving the product function $\prod_{i=1}^{n}(x_i+1)$ and discuss their applications in the knapsack setting.

</details>


### [124] [Distribution-Agnostic Robust Trajectory Optimization via Chance-Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13605)
*Yashdeep Chaudhary,Roberto Armellin,Harry Holt,Marco Sagliano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机会约束强化学习的分布无关鲁棒轨迹优化框架。该框架先离线计算确定性标称轨迹，再通过强化学习学习一个结构化的仿射闭环校正律（包含前馈控制调整和时变反馈增益）来鲁棒化该基线。通过基于rollout的上尾分位数经验性地强制执行概率可行性，并通过协方差可行性惩罚来调节终端分散度。在两个截然不同的轨迹设计问题（三维多脉冲地火转移和随机大气精确定点火箭着陆）上评估了该框架，结果表明其在保持概率可行性的同时在上尾燃料成本上具有竞争力，且同一鲁棒化框架可跨异构航天器轨迹规划问题迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒轨迹优化方法通常假设特定的不确定性分布（如高斯分布），而实际航天任务中的不确定性可能来自多种未知分布。作者旨在开发一种不依赖特定分布假设的鲁棒轨迹优化方法，能够在仅可采样不确定性的条件下保证概率可行性。

Method: 1) 先离线计算确定性标称轨迹；2) 使用强化学习学习一个结构化仿射闭环校正律，包含前馈控制调整和时变反馈增益；3) 通过基于rollout的上尾分位数经验性地强制执行概率可行性约束；4) 通过协方差可行性惩罚调节终端分散度。仅需要不确定性可采样，无需分布假设。

Result: 在三维多脉冲地火转移问题中，该方法在高斯不确定性下与最新鲁棒轨迹优化基准方法相比具有竞争力的上尾燃料成本，且在均匀不确定性和未见过的过程扰动下仍表现良好。在随机大气精确定点火箭着陆问题中，该方法成功迁移至短时域连续推力场景，证明了框架的通用性。

Conclusion: 该框架在不假设特定不确定性分布的情况下实现了概率可行的鲁棒轨迹优化，且其核心随机控制结构可跨异构航天器轨迹规划问题直接迁移，无需重新设计。

Abstract: This paper presents a distribution-agnostic robust trajectory-optimization framework based on chance-constrained reinforcement learning. The uncertainty is represented here through initial conditions and process noise, with the only requirement being that it can be sampled. A deterministic nominal trajectory is first computed offline, and reinforcement learning is then used only to robustify that baseline through a structured affine closed-loop correction law comprising a feedforward control adjustment and time-varying feedback gains. Probabilistic feasibility is enforced empirically through rollout-based upper-tail quantiles, while terminal dispersion is regulated through covariance-feasibility penalties. The framework is assessed on two materially different trajectory design problems. The flagship case study is a three-dimensional multi-impulse Earth-Mars transfer, where the learned policy is benchmarked against a recent robust trajectory-optimization reference under Gaussian uncertainty and then evaluated under bounded uniform uncertainty and under process disturbances not seen during training. The second case study is a stochastic atmospheric pinpoint rocket landing problem, used to assess portability to a short-horizon continuous-thrust setting with drag, mass depletion, and glide-slope constraints. The results show that the proposed framework can remain competitive in upper-tail fuel cost while preserving probabilistic feasibility, and that the same robustification scaffold can be carried across heterogeneous spacecraft trajectory planning problems without redesign of its core stochastic-control structure.

</details>

### [125] [Estimating Individualized Treatment Effects in Acute Ischemic Stroke with Causal Transformation Models (TRAM-DAG): A Multi-Centre Observational Study with External RCT Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12623)
*Oliver Dürr,Lisa Herzog,Pascal Bühler,Susanne Wegener,Beate Sick*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于有向无环图的因果变换模型（TRAM-DAG）从观察性数据中估计急性缺血性卒中患者的个体化治疗效果（ITE），以识别机械取栓相较于溶栓获益最大的患者群体，并在MR CLEAN试验人群中进行验证，结果表明ITE估计值的平均值与试验报告的ATE一致，且能正确对患者结局进行排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准医学要求超越平均治疗效果（ATE），提供个体化治疗效果（ITE）估计以支持临床治疗决策。在急性缺血性卒中中，需要确定哪些个体患者能从机械取栓中比溶栓获益更多。

Method: 采用因果变换模型（TRAM-DAG）拟合于MAGIC多中心卒中观察性数据，通过限制NIHSS评分≥6的子群体以匹配MR CLEAN试验的纳入标准，进而估计MR CLEAN人群中每位患者的ITE。

Result: ITE估计值的平均值与MR CLEAN试验报告的ATE一致；ITE估计值能够正确将试验患者按良好结局（三个月mRS≤2）的观察频率进行排序。

Conclusion: 因果模型如TRAM-DAG可用于个性化卒中治疗决策，弥合观察性证据与临床试验之间的差距。

Abstract: Personalized medicine in acute ischemic stroke requires moving beyond average treatment effects (ATE) to individualized treatment effect (ITE) estimates to support treatment decisions. In acute ischemic stroke, mechanical thrombectomy has been shown to be more effective on average than lysis in randomized controlled trials (RCTs), such as the MR CLEAN study. We aim to identify which individual patients benefit most from mechanical thrombectomy compared to lysis. The outcome of interest is the modified Rankin Scale (mRS) at three months, an ordinal measure of functional disability (0: no symptoms, 6: death). We demonstrate that causal transformation models on directed acyclic graphs (TRAM-DAG) can be used for ITE estimation after being fitted on observational MAGIC multi-center stroke patient data. To ensure comparability with the MR CLEAN population, which we use for validation, we train the TRAM-DAG on a MAGIC sub-population with NIHSS at admission >= 6, corresponding to one inclusion criterion of MR CLEAN. The fitted model is then used to estimate ITEs for stroke patients in the MR CLEAN population. While these ITE estimates cannot be confirmed experimentally, we show that their average is consistent with the trial's reported ATE. Furthermore, the ITE estimates correctly rank trial patients by their observed frequency of a good outcome (mRS at three months <= 2). These findings support the use of causal models like TRAM-DAG for personalized decision-making in stroke care and highlight their ability to bridge the gap between observational evidence and clinical trials.

</details>


### [126] [The Persistent Non-Response Bias in a Sample-Matched Poll for the 2024 U.S. Presidential Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12889)
*Jay Chooi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文运用数据缺陷相关框架分析2024年美国总统大选民调偏差，发现特朗普选民的无应答偏差持续存在（ρ=-0.0030），哈里斯选民存在正向应答偏差。民调误差与州人口规模成比例，大样本下有效样本量缩减超99%。提出的选举前偏差校正估计将RMSE从0.13降至0.05。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2024年大选民调再次出现系统性偏差，尽管预测民主党领先但特朗普获胜，与2016年情况类似。本研究旨在量化并解释这种持续性民调偏差的来源，并开发预选举偏差校正方法。

Method: 基于数据缺陷相关框架，使用合作选举研究（CES）的60,000受访者数据，分析无应答偏差和应答偏差。通过比较2016年（ρ=-0.0045）与2024年（ρ=-0.0030）的数据缺陷相关性，检验样本匹配后的偏差持续性。提出利用历史数据缺陷相关性和投票率信息构建选举前偏差校正估计量。

Result: 特朗普选民的无应答偏差在2024年持续存在（ρ=-0.0030），哈里斯选民存在正向应答偏差。民调误差与州人口规模成比例，大州有效样本量缩减超99%。提出的预选举偏差校正估计量将RMSE从0.13降至0.05，优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 民调偏差具有系统性且可预测的特征，利用历史数据缺陷相关性可以在选举前有效校正偏差，显著降低预测误差，为改进民调方法论提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Donald Trump won the 2024 US Presidential Election despite polls predicting a Democratic lead, echoing the polling miss in 2016. Using the data defect correlation framework, we revisit the 60,000-respondent Cooperative Election Study and find that non-response bias for Trump voters persists on the same order of magnitude ($ρ=-0.0030$ vs $-0.0045$ in 2016) even under sample-matching to the US adult population. We additionally find evidence of positive response bias for Harris voters after adjusting for turnout. Consistent with findings in 2016, polling errors scale with state population size, and larger samples produce greater departures from conventional confidence intervals, with reductions of effective sample size exceeding 99% in the largest states. We propose a pre-election bias correction estimator informed by historical data defect correlations and turnout rates that decreases RMSE from 0.13 to 0.05 using only prior election data, comparable to post-election weighting (RMSE 0.09).

</details>


### [127] [Stochastic Modeling of Composite Interfaces: Sensitivity to Spatial Correlation and Bayesian Identification from Standard Fracture Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13019)
*Elton Donfack-Siewe,Sylvain Dubreuil,Christian Fagiano,Jérôme Morio,Jean-Philippe Navarro*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机有限元框架，通过空间相关随机场表示层间界面变异性，结合近似贝叶斯计算方法从实验数据提取关键参数（相关长度），以提高航空复合材料加筋脱粘可靠性评估的精度，实现高保真虚拟测试与不确定性预测管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合材料结构中的不确定性难以数值化处理，现有方法在加筋脱粘等失效模式的可靠性评估上精度不足，缺乏从实验数据中提取关键随机参数的有效手段，难以支撑工业级损伤容限设计。

Method: 构建随机有限元框架，采用空间相关随机场表征层合部件间的界面变异性；通过I型与II型断裂试验的参数化研究，识别相关长度为变异性主要驱动因素；利用近似贝叶斯计算方法从实验断裂数据中提取相关长度参数。

Result: 相关长度是观测变异性的主要驱动因素，而协方差核的正则性影响甚微；所提出的近似贝叶斯计算方法能够从实验数据中有效提取这一关键参数。

Conclusion: 该框架为高保真虚拟测试和复合材料机身损伤容限设计中的不确定性预测管理提供了一条稳健的技术路径，具备工业应用价值。

Abstract: To enable a numerical handling of uncertainties in composite structures, this work presents a stochastic finite-element framework aimed at improving the reliability assessment of aerospace composites, with particular attention to stiffener debonding. By representing interface variability between laminate parts with spatially correlated random fields, the method aims at considering scattering effect at a higher scale of simulation and testing. A parametric study carried out on standardized Mode I and Mode II fracture tests reveals that the correlation length is the primary driver of observed variability, while the regularity of the covariance kernel has only a marginal impact. To guarantee industrial relevance, we demonstrate that this key parameter can be extracted from experimental fracture data using an Approximate Bayesian Computation approach. The proposed methodology therefore offers a robust route to high-fidelity virtual testing and to the predictive management of uncertainties in the design of damage-tolerant composite airframes.

</details>


### [128] [Efficient Estimation of A-basis and B-Basis Value under Epistemic Uncertainty using Importance Sampling and Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13094)
*Elton Donfack-Siewe,Jérôme Morio,Sylvain Dubreuil,Jean-Philippe Navarro,Christian Fagiano*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在混合认知-偶然不确定性下，利用确定性数值模型估计保守设计分位数（如A/B基准值）的方法，通过重要性采样和控制变量法实现无偏一致估计，代理模型仅作为方差缩减工具，并可在不增加成本下获得Sobol敏感性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空航天等安全关键领域需要保守分位数估计（如A/B基准值）以保证可靠性，但传统方法依赖实验数据，成本高昂。本文致力于利用已验证的确定性数值模型来替代实验，在有限计算预算下实现具有统计保证的保守设计分位数估计。

Method: 在混合偶然-认知不确定性框架下，同时考虑材料数据有限性、有限采样效应和替代模型误差。采用重要性采样和控制变量法实现高精度高效率估计，代理模型仅作为方差缩减工具以保证无偏一致性。通过显式整合所有不确定性来源，提供A/B基准值的数值估计方案，并可零成本获得Sobol敏感性指标。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在结构模型上具有可靠性和计算效率，在大规模工业仿真中的应用证实其适用于航空航天认证流程。

Conclusion: 该方法为安全关键领域提供了一种有效的数值替代方案，能够在有限计算预算下获得具有统计保证的保守分位数估计，且代理模型仅作为方差缩减手段保证了估计的无偏性，Sobol敏感性指标有助于识别主导的认知不确定性来源。

Abstract: In aerospace certification and other safety-critical domains, conservative quantile estimation such as A- and B-basis values is essential to guarantee reliability. While these metrics are traditionally derived from experimental campaigns, this work focuses on their estimation using a validated deterministic numerical model. The problem is formulated under mixed aleatory-epistemic uncertainty, accounting for limited material data, finite sampling effects, and surrogate modeling errors. We propose a methodology for estimating conservative design quantiles with statistical guarantees under mixed uncertainties. The proposed method leverages importance sampling and control variates to achieve accurate and efficient estimates within a fixed computational budget. One key point is the surrogate model's role solely as a variance reduction device, which guarantees unbiased and consistent quantile estimation. By explicitly integrating all sources of uncertainty, the proposed framework provides a numerical alternative to estimate A-basis and B-Basis. Furthermore, Sobol-based sensitivity indices are obtained at no additional cost, offering insight into the dominant epistemic sources. Numerical experiments on structural models demonstrate the method's reliability and computational efficiency. In particular, the application to large-scale industrial simulations confirms its suitability for aerospace certification workflows and highlights its relevance for real world engineering environments.

</details>


### [129] [Scaling Demand-Side Flexibility Through Dynamic Tariffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13401)
*Lucas Brylle,Niels Andersen,Henrik Madsen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文论证了通过动态电价激励的隐式需求侧灵活性（DSF）是解决丹麦配电网因电气化和可再生能源整合带来的运营挑战（如备用容量不足、过载、电压不稳及投资需求增加）的最具可扩展性和成本效益的方案，基于实证数据可节省每个受限变电站1300-4800万丹麦克朗的电网加固成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丹麦配电网在电气化和可再生能源整合过程中面临备用容量不足、设备过载退化、电压不稳定以及日益增长的基础设施投资需求等运营挑战，亟需可扩展且经济的解决方案。

Method: 通过实证消费数据分析价格响应行为，结合丹麦Market Model 3.0和Tariff Model 3.0等监管框架，以及经济分析方法，对比显式和隐式需求侧灵活性机制的可扩展性和成本效益。

Result: 隐式DSF机制可使每个受限变电站通过延迟或避免电网加固节省1300-4800万丹麦克朗；额外价值流包括避免峰值发电成本、减少并网延迟和降低停电风险。

Conclusion: 隐式需求侧灵活性（动态电价）是实现可扩展、收益中性的灵活性解决方案的必要路径，能够延迟昂贵的电网加固同时维持系统可靠性；动态电价可将实时电网约束信号传递给消费者，反映配电网在时间和空间维度的实际容量状态。

Abstract: The ongoing electrification and integration of renewable energy sources in Denmark's distribution grids pose significant operational challenges, including insufficient reserve capacity, component degradation due to overload, voltage instability, and increasing infrastructure investment requirements. This article argues that implicit demand-side flexibility (DSF) incentivized through dynamic tariffs offers the most scalable and cost-effective approach to address these challenges in a modern distribution network. We demonstrate that while explicit flexibility mechanisms provide operational certainty, they cannot scale to address system-wide congestion across heterogeneous customer bases. Drawing on empirical consumption data showing strong price-responsive behavior, varying prices due to, e.g., regulatory frameworks including the Danish Market Model 3.0 and Tariff Model 3.0, and economic analysis, we demonstrate potential grid savings of 13--48 million DKK per constrained substation through deferred or avoided reinforcement. We argue that implicit DSF mechanisms represent the necessary pathway for revenue-neutral scalable flexibility solutions that can defer costly grid reinforcements while maintaining system reliability. Beyond direct grid savings, additional value streams include avoided peak generation costs, reduced connection delays, and lower outage risk, further strengthening the economic case. Critically, dynamic tariffs offer the mechanism through which real-time grid constraints can be communicated to consumers, enabling price signals that accurately reflect the actual state of the capacity of the distribution network at any given point in time and space.

</details>

### [130] [Lower bounds on expressions depending on the functions {\boldmath$\varphi(n)$}, {\boldmath$ψ(n)$} and {\boldmath$σ(n)$}, III](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12484)
*S. I. Dimitrov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究算术函数 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 相关各种表达式的下界，并建立了若干显式估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为算术函数 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 的相关表达式提供精确的下界估计，以深化对这些基本数论函数性质的定量理解。

Method: 采用解析数论和初等数论的方法，对 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 的各种组合表达式进行下界推导，并给出显式（explicit）的数值估计形式。

Result: 建立了多个关于 φ(n)、ψ(n) 和 σ(n) 的表达式的显式下界估计。

Conclusion: 本文为相关算术函数的下界研究提供了新的显式结果，有助于更精确地刻画这些数论函数的分布与增长行为。

Abstract: This work is concerned with the study of lower bounds for various expressions related to the arithmetic functions $\varphi(n)$, $ψ(n)$ and $σ(n)$. Several explicit estimates are established.

</details>


### [131] [Root Clusters and Multiclusters over Imperfect Hilbertian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12660)
*Shubham Jaiswal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将根簇理论从完美域推广到一般域（不必完美），引入了根簇大小、多簇大小、根容量、多根容量、上升指数、上升正规指数、交指示子、交正规指示子、复合指示子、复合正规指示子等概念，并在Hilbert域上建立了这些广义概念的逆问题结果，推广了先前在数域上的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将根簇理论从完美域推广到非完美的一般域，并建立相关逆问题在Hilbert域上的存在性结果。

Method: 通过引入多个新概念（根簇大小、根容量、上升指数、交指示子、复合指示子等）及其广义形式，并研究它们在域扩张中的性质，进而建立Hilbert域上的逆问题。

Result: 在给定Hilbert域上，证明了对于给定次数、簇大小和多簇大小的多项式的存在性，以及对于给定根容量和多根容量的扩张的存在性。

Conclusion: 将根簇理论及相关逆问题的结果从完美域（及数域）成功推广到了更一般的域（含非完美域）和Hilbert域上。

Abstract: We extend the theory of root clusters from perfect fields to general fields which are not necessarily perfect. We introduce the following notions for field extensions over any given base field and study their interesting properties: root cluster size, multicluster size and their generalizations root capacity, multiroot capacity; ascending index, ascending normal index and their generalizations intersection indicium, intersection normal indicium; compositum indicium and compositum normal indicium. We establish our results on the Inverse problems for these generalized notions over Hilbertian fields which generalizes our earlier results which were over number fields. In particular, we show over a given Hilbertian field, the existence of a polynomial for given degree, cluster size and multicluster size and existence of an extension for given root capacity and multiroot capacity with respect to that polynomial.

</details>


### [132] [An AI Proof of 18-Variable Undecidability for Diophantine Equations over $\mathbb Z[i]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12776)
*Yuchen Ding,Junfeng Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过AI辅助证明，将高斯整数上丢番图方程不可判定性的未知数个数从20降至18，主要改进了清除分母变量的处理和非零条件的吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Matiyasevich和Sun的20未知数定理，进一步降低高斯整数上多项式方程可解性判定问题的未知数个数下界。

Method: 沿用Matiyasevich和Sun的有理性判据和整数检验框架，通过两个整数条件替代清除分母变量，并利用单变量技巧(2R+1)(3R+1)将剩余的非零条件吸收进关系组合引理，从而节省两个变量。

Result: 证明了不存在算法能够判定高斯整数上含18个未知数的多项式方程是否有解，将未知数个数从20改进到18。

Conclusion: 高斯整数上丢番图方程的可解性判定问题在18个未知数时已是不可判定的。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI proof that there is no algorithm deciding whether a polynomial equation over the Gaussian integers in $18$ unknowns has a solution. The proof improves the $20$-unknown theorem of Matiyasevich and Sun. It follows their rationality criterion and integer test, but saves two variables: the clearing-denominator variable is avoided by imposing two integer conditions, and the remaining nonzero condition is absorbed into the relation-combining lemma by the one-variable gadget $(2R+1)(3R+1)$.

</details>


### [133] [Splitting of Polynomial Families via Galois Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12810)
*Tianhao Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过经典Galois理论和Chebotarev密度定理，在仿射正规簇上研究有限域上多项式族的分裂行为，避免了étale拓扑的繁重工具。主要工作是推广Slavov关于多项式平方值独立性的结果到n次幂剩余情形，并将独立性条件重新解释为Kummer扩张的互素线性不交条件，最后简要说明如何将结果翻译到étale基本群的语言中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前有限域上多项式族的分裂行为研究依赖于Grothendieck étale拓扑等重工具，且Slavov关于多项式平方值独立性的结果尚未被从更本质的Galois理论角度解释和推广到n次幂剩余情形。

Method: 采用经典Galois理论和Chebotarev密度定理，在仿射正规簇的框架下建立理论基础；利用Kummer扩张的线性不交性重新表述独立性条件；最后将结果推广到étale基本群的语言中，将底空间推广为$\mathbb{F}_q$上有限型的几何整正规概形。

Result: 在$q\equiv 1 \pmod n$条件下，将多项式平方值独立性的结果成功推广到n次幂剩余情形，并将独立性条件等价地刻画为Kummer扩张的互素线性不交条件。

Conclusion: 通过经典工具即可避免étale拓扑的繁重技术，推广并更深层地解释了Slavov的结果；该方法还可自然地翻译到étale基本群的现代语言中，推广到更一般的基概形。

Abstract: We study the splitting behavior of parametrized families of polynomials over finite fields through a geometric and Galois-theoretic approach. While the underlying techniques are widely considered folklore in arithmetic geometry, they have rarely been written down explicitly. To maximize accessibility, we develop a framework based on classical Galois theory and the Chebotarev Density Theorem over an affine normal variety, avoiding the heavy machinery of Grothendieck's étale topology.
  The primary goal is to extend and conceptually explain a recent result by Slavov, which established the condition for square values of several polynomials over a finite field to be independent. In the case where $q\equiv 1 \pmod n$, we generalize this phenomenon to $n$-th power residues, and reframe this independence condition as the natural condition on Kummer extensions to be mutually linearly disjoint. Finally, we briefly mention how these results can be translated into the modern language of étale fundamental groups, generalizing the base to geometrically integral, normal schemes of finite type over $\mathbb{F}_q$.

</details>


### [134] [Effective Estimates for a Class of Farey Fraction Sums and Bounds for Mundici-Type Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12907)
*Anji Dong,Huy Xuan Nguyen,Vi Anh Nguyen,Alexandru Zaharescu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Mundici关于全区间连续间距的猜想（D₂(Q)·Q²/log Q < 3）推广到子区间和h-间距情形，获得了相应的Mundici型界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mundici猜想已获证，本文希望将其结论推广到更一般的设定（子区间与h-间距），以拓展该猜想的适用范围。

Method: 通过分析子区间上Farey分数连续间距的平方和以及h-间距情形，进行推广证明。

Result: 在子区间和h-间距两种设定下均获得了Mundici型上界。

Conclusion: 成功将Mundici猜想的结论从全区间连续间距推广至子区间和h-间距情形。

Abstract: Let $D_{2}(Q)$ denote the sum of squared distances between consecutive Farey fractions in the full interval $(0, 1]$. Daniele Mundici conjectured that $C(Q):=D_{2}(Q)\cdot Q^2/\log Q$ is less than 3 for all $Q\geq 2$, which is confirmed true in \cite{DLN2026}. In this paper, we generalize this result to subintervals of $(0, 1]$ and to $h$-spacings. As applications, we obtain Mundici-type bounds in these two settings, extending the full-interval consecutive-spacing case of Mundici's conjecture.

</details>


### [135] [Multi-target hyperbolic sieves and elliptic trace obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13018)
*Pantelimon Stănică,Erik Mulder,Markus Hittmeir*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究半素数N=pq的二次筛集H_a(N;ℓ)的局部筛法性质，给出了两个靶标情形下并集基数的精确公式（涉及四次特征和与椭圆曲线Frobenius迹），以及多靶标情形下的误差估计，并推广到r-幂约束情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对确定性整数分解中提出的双靶标本原根情形，需要系统分析多个线性形式的同时筛法性质，并给出完整的局部理论刻画。

Method: 利用Legendre符号χ构造四次特征和K(A,B)，将其与射影光滑亏格1曲线Y^2=(X^2-A)(X^2-B)的Frobenius迹（Hasse-Weil界）关联，通过Hasse-Weil定理给出O(√ℓ)误差；多靶标情形采用并集估计与CRT乘积界；幂约束情形通过初等对合论证确定精确基数。

Result: (1) 双靶标并集基数|H_a(N;ℓ)∪H_b(N;ℓ)|可用四次特征和K(A,B)精确表达，主项为3ℓ/4，误差由椭圆曲线Frobenius迹控制；(2) k个系数情形的并集基数估计为ℓ(1-2^{-k})，误差为(k-1+2^{-k})√ℓ+k；(3) 特殊形式N=u^rv的r-幂约束筛集H_{a,r}(N;ℓ)的精确基数通过对合论证确定。

Conclusion: 将局部筛法问题转化为显式的有限域陈述，可以通过验证局部测试来确认，负Frobenius迹解释了此前点态界3ℓ/4+1的反例。

Abstract: Let $N=pq$ be a semiprime and let $\ell\nmid Na$ be an odd prime. The hyperbolic sieve set $H_a(N;\ell)=\{ax+Nx^{-1}:x\in\mathbb F_\ell^*\}$ contains the residue of the linear form $ap+q$ modulo $\ell$ and has exact cardinality $(\ell+χ(aN))/2$, where $χ$ is the Legendre symbol modulo $\ell$. We study simultaneous sieving for several linear forms and give a complete local analysis of the two-target primitive-root case proposed in connection with deterministic integer factorization. For two distinct coefficients $a,b$, with $A=4aN$ and $B=4bN$, we prove an exact formula for $|H_a(N;\ell)\cup H_b(N;\ell)|$ in terms of the degree-four character sum \[ K(A,B)=\sum_{z\in\mathbb F_\ell}χ((z^2-A)(z^2-B)).\] For a smooth projective genus-one curve $E/\mathbb{F}_\ell$, we write $t_E=\ell+1-\#E(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$ for its Frobenius trace. With this convention, $K(A,B)$ is the Frobenius trace, up to sign and an additive constant, of the genus-one curve $Y^2=(X^2-A)(X^2-B)$. Hence Hasse--Weil gives a uniform $O(\sqrt\ell)$ error from the main term $3\ell/4$, and negative traces explain the counterexamples to the pointwise bound $3\ell/4+1$. We also prove a multi-target estimate \[\left|\left|\bigcup_{j=1}^k H_{a_j}(N;\ell)\right|-\ell(1-2^{-k})\right|\le (k-1+2^{-k})\sqrt\ell+k\] for distinct coefficients $a_1,\ldots,a_k$, together with the corresponding CRT product bound. Finally, for special-shape inputs $N=u^rv$, we study the $r$-power-constrained image $H_{a,r}(N;\ell)$ and determine its exact size by an elementary involution argument. These results recast the proposed local sieve questions as explicit finite-field statements with verified local tests.

</details>


### [136] [Ramanujan's and Lim's Identities and Harmonic Maass--Jacobi Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13173)
*Kathrin Bringmann,Rajat Gupta,Badri Vishal Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入Eisenstein级数的Jacobi模拟Eichler积分，研究Lim关于奇zeta值Ramanujan恒等式的推广，证明这些对象是（奇异）调和Maass-Jacobi形式，并描述其非全纯部分、建立Ramanujan型反演公式，以及研究其在Maass升降算子和扭点下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Lim关于奇zeta值的Ramanujan恒等式，并建立负权情况下Eisenstein级数Eichler积分的Jacobi模拟的模性框架。

Method: 构造负权Eisenstein级数的Jacobi模拟Eichler积分的显式完备化，将其嵌入调和Maass-Jacobi形式框架；利用Maass升降算子和扭点分析其结构。

Result: 证明了这些Jacobi模拟Eichler积分是（奇异）调和Maass-Jacobi形式，描述了其非全纯部分（用Eichler积分表示），建立了Ramanujan型反演公式，并揭示了其在Maass升降算子和扭点下的行为。

Conclusion: 成功将Lim的Ramanujan恒等式推广到Jacobi形式语境，为奇zeta值的模性解释提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study an extension of Ramanujan's identities for odd zeta values by Lim and introduce Jacobi analogues of classical Eichler integrals of Eisenstein series. In negative weight we construct explicit completions and embed these objects into a modular framework by showing that they are (singular) harmonic Maass--Jacobi forms. We further describe their non-holomorphic parts in terms of Eichler integrals, establish Ramanujan-type inversion formulas, and study their behavior under the Maass raising and lowering operators and at torsion points.

</details>


### [137] [Discrete Fourier Transform Approach to Cyclically Covering Subspaces of $\mathbb{F}^n_q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13307)
*Yangcheng Li,Pingzhi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域F_q^n上的循环覆盖子空间的最大余维数h_q(n)，通过离散傅里叶变换给出了当gcd(q,n)=1时h_q(n)=0的充要条件，并推广到余维数为k的情形。在q和n均为素数且n>q、q为模n原根的情况下给出了统一刻画，证明了h_2(n)≥2、h_q(n)=0（q≠2），以及h_3(n)≥1（特定条件）。在广义黎曼假设下，证明了存在无穷多个n使得h_q(n)=0。还给出了h_q(mn)的不等式、通过Galois下降得到h_{q^m}(n)≤h_q(n)，推广了达到上界⌊log_q(n)⌋的构造，并在GRH下得到了q=2,3的平均下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环覆盖子空间的概念源于循环码和覆盖问题，动机是理解子空间在所有循环移位下能否覆盖全空间，以及这种覆盖能力与余维数之间的关系。通过Fourier变换和循环码的联系，揭示其代数结构。

Method: 使用离散傅里叶变换（DFT）导出充要条件；利用原根和素数的数论性质进行分类刻画；运用Galois下降得到不同域扩张下的关系；借助广义黎曼假设分析存在性和平均下界。

Result: (1) gcd(q,n)=1时，h_q(n)=0等价于存在全重码字的循环码；(2) q,n为素数且n>q、q为模n原根时，h_2(n)≥2，h_q(n)=0 (q≠2)；(3) 素数n>3且ord_n(3)为奇数时，h_3(n)≥1；(4) GRH下，存在无穷多素数n使h_q(n)=0；(5) 不等式h_q(mn)≥max{h_q(m),h_q(n)}和≥h_q(m)+h_q(n)；(6) Galois下降：h_{q^m}(n)≤h_q(n)；(7) 推广构造达到上界⌊log_q(n)⌋；(8) GRH下得到q=2,3的平均下界。

Conclusion: 本文对循环覆盖子空间问题给出了较为完整的代数-数论刻画，建立了与循环码、离散傅里叶变换、原根和广义黎曼假设的深刻联系，并为h_q(n)的上下界提供了多种方法和结果。

Abstract: Let $q$ be a prime power and $n$ a positive integer. A subspace \( U \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^n \) is called cyclically covering if the union of all its cyclic shifts covers the whole space \( \mathbb{F}_q^n \). Let \( h_q(n) \) denote the maximum possible codimension of such a subspace. When \(\gcd(q,n)=1\), we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for \(h_q(n)=0\) via Discrete Fourier Transforms, and prove this equality is equivalent to the existence of full-weight codewords in cyclic codes of \(\mathbb{F}_q^n\). We also characterize codimension-$k$ cyclically covering subspaces.
  Based on these results, we give a unified characterization of \(h_q(n)\) in the case where $q$ and $n$ are primes with \(n>q\) and $q$ being a primitive root modulo $n$. Specifically, \(h_2(n) \geq 2\) and \(h_q(n) = 0\) for \(q \neq 2\). We prove that \(h_3(n) \ge 1\) for every prime \(n > 3\) with odd \(\operatorname{ord}_n(3)\). Moreover, for any prime \(q > 3\), the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis implies the existence of infinitely many primes \(n > q\) such that $q$ is not a primitive root modulo $n$ and \(h_q(n) = 0\). We provide algebraic interpretations for the inequalities \(h_q(mn)\ge\max\{h_q(m),h_q(n)\}\) and \(h_q(mn)\ge h_q(m)+h_q(n)\). Using Galois descent, we prove \(h_{q^m}(n)\le h_q(n)\). Furthermore, we generalize a class of constructions that achieve the upper bound \(\lfloor\log_q(n)\rfloor\). Finally, under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, we obtain average lower bounds of \(h_q(n)\) for $q=2,3$.

</details>


### [138] [Some new modular Nahm sums of ranks 3 and 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13590)
*Zhineng Cao,Liuquan Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发现了六个新的模Nahm和族，其中两个是秩3的，四个是秩4的，并通过建立Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式将它们表示为无穷乘积来证明其模性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展模Nahm和的研究，发现新的模形式族，特别是在秩3和秩4的情况下，丰富该领域的已知结果。

Method: 通过修改Zagier的秩3例子得到两个族；对Milas和Wang研究的秩3 tadpole Nahm和施加lift-dual操作导出三个秩4族；使用常数项方法发现另一个秩4族；建立Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式将Nahm和表示为模无穷乘积以证明模性。

Result: 成功发现了六个新的模Nahm和族（两个秩3，四个秩4），并证明了它们的模性。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了模Nahm和的已知家族，并展示了多种构造新模形式的方法（修改、lift-dual操作、常数项方法）的有效性。

Abstract: We discover six new families of modular Nahm sums in ranks 3 and 4. Two of them are rank three sums obtained by modifying two of Zagier's rank three examples. Three rank four families are derived by applying the lift-dual operation to the rank three tadpole Nahm sums studied by Milas and Wang, while the other rank four family is found by the constant term method. To prove modularity, we establish Rogers-Ramanujan type identities that express these Nahm sums as infinite products which are modular.

</details>


### [139] [Split primes and the Elekes-Rónyai problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13619)
*Cosmin Pohoata*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了存在绝对常数c>0和任意大的有限实数集A，使得集合{x+y+(x-y)^2: x,y∈A}的大小不超过|A|^{2-c}。由于该多项式既非加法型也非乘法型，这一结果构成了Elekes-Rónyai问题的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Elekes-Rónyai问题探讨了关于多项式在有限集合上的像的大小的界，特别是对于既非加法型也非乘法型的多项式。该论文旨在构造一个反例，表明对于这类多项式，|P(A,A)|可以远小于|A|^2。

Method: 论文通过构造特定的实数集A和选取多项式P(x,y)=x+y+(x-y)^2，并证明对于该多项式存在一个绝对常数c>0，使得|P(A,A)| ≤ |A|^{2-c}成立，从而提供了一个具体的反例。

Result: 存在绝对常数c>0和任意大的有限实数集A，使得对于多项式x+y+(x-y)^2，其像集的大小不超过|A|^{2-c}。

Conclusion: 该结果对Elekes-Rónyai问题给出了否定的回答，即对于既非加法型也非乘法型的多项式，不能保证|P(A,A)|达到|A|^2的量级。

Abstract: There exist an absolute constant $c>0$ and arbitrarily large finite sets $A\subset \mathbb{R}$ with $$\left| \left\{x+y+(x-y)^2:\ x, y \in A\right\}\right| \le|A|^{2-c}.$$ Since $x+y+(x-y)^2 \in \mathbb{R}[x,y]$ is a polynomial which is neither additive nor multiplicative, this provides a counterexample for the Elekes-Rónyai problem.

</details>
